## Settings

**File > Settings...** opens a settings window (Apply / OK / Cancel) holding
the app's options - currently the UI theme (all installed AtlantaFX themes,
applied immediately on Apply/OK and persisted). Settings that cannot apply
live are marked as taking effect after restart.

the app's options - currently the UI theme (all installed AtlantaFX themes,
applied immediately on Apply/OK and persisted). Settings that cannot apply
live are marked as taking effect after restart.

the app's options - currently the UI theme (all installed AtlantaFX themes,
applied immediately on Apply/OK and persisted). Settings that cannot apply
live are marked as taking effect after restart.
